			that they might not enter into temptation.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:11} % *ALMA-31-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-11}

			Yea, in fine, they did pervert the ways of the Lord in very many instances.
			Therefore for this cause Alma and his brethren went into the land
			to preach the word unto them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:12} % *ALMA-31-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-12}

			Now when they had came into the land,
			behold, to their astonishment they found that the Zoramites had built synagogues
			and that they did gather themselves together on one day of the week,
			which day they did call the day of the Lord,
			and they did worship after a manner
			which Alma and his brethren had never beheld.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:13} % *ALMA-31-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-13}

			For they had a place built up in the center of their synagogue,
			a place of standing which was high above the head;
			and the top thereof would only admit one person.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:14} % *ALMA-31-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-14}

			Therefore whosoever desired to worship
			must go forth and stand upon the top thereof
			and stretch forth his hands towards the heavens
			and cry with a loud voice, saying:

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:15} % *ALMA-31-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-15}

			Holy holy God, we believe that thou art God.
			And we believe that thou art holy,
			and that thou wast a spirit
			and that thou art a spirit
			and that thou wilt be a spirit forever.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:16} % *ALMA-31-16 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-16}

			Holy God, we believe that thou hast separated us from our brethren.
			And we do not believe in the tradition of our brethren
			which was handed down to them by the childishness of their fathers,
			but we believe that thou hast \hyperref[ELECTION]{elected} us to be thy holy children,
			and also thou hast made it known unto us that there shall be no Christ.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:17} % *ALMA-31-17 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-17}

			But thou art the same yesterday, today, and forever.
			And thou hast \hyperref[ELECTION]{elected} us that we shall be saved,
			whilst all around us are \hyperref[ELECTION]{elected} to be cast by thy wrath down to hell,
			for the which holiness, O God, we thank thee.
			And we also thank thee that thou hast \hyperref[ELECTION]{elected} us
			that we may not be led away after the foolish traditions of our brethren,
			which doth bind them down to a belief of Christ,
			which doth lead their hearts to wander far from thee, our God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:18} % *ALMA-31-18 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-18}

			And again we thank thee, O God,
			that we are a chosen and a holy people.
			Amen.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:19} % *ALMA-31-19 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-19}

			Now it came to pass that
			after Alma and his brethren and his sons had heard these prayers,
			they were astonished beyond all measure;

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:20} % *ALMA-31-20 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-20}

			for behold, every man did go forth and offer up these same prayers.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:21} % *ALMA-31-21 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-21}

			Now the place was called by them Rameumptom,
			which being interpreted is the holy stand.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:22} % *ALMA-31-22 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-22}

			Now from this stand they did offer up every man the selfsame prayer unto God,
			thanking their God that they were chosen of him
			and that he had not led them away after the tradition of their brethren
			and that their hearts were not stolen away
			to believe in things to come, which they knew nothing about.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:23} % *ALMA-31-23 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-23}

			Now when the people had all offered up thanks after their manner,
			they returned to their homes, never speaking of their God again
			until they had assembled themselves together again to the holy stand
			to offer up thanks after their manner.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:24} % *ALMA-31-24 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-24}

			Now when Alma saw this, his heart was grieved;
			for he saw that they were a wicked and a perverse people.
			Yea, he saw that their hearts were set upon gold and upon silver
			and upon all manner of fine goods.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:25} % *ALMA-31-25 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-25}

			Yea, and he also saw
			that their hearts were lifted up unto great boasting in their pride.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:26} % *ALMA-31-26 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-26}

			And he lifted up his voice to heaven and cried, saying:
			O how long, O Lord, wilt thou suffer
			that thy servants shall dwell here below in the flesh
			to behold such gross wickedness among the children of men?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:27} % *ALMA-31-27 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-27}

			Behold, O God, they cry unto thee,
			and yet their hearts are swallowed up in their pride.
			Behold, O God, they cry unto thee with their mouths
			while they are puffed up, even to greatness, with the vain things of the world.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:28} % *ALMA-31-28 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-28}

			Behold, O my God, their costly apparel
			and their ringlets and their bracelets and their ornaments of gold
			and all their precious things which they are ornamented with---
			and behold, their hearts are set upon them.
			And yet they cry unto thee and say:
			We thank thee, O God,
			for we are a chosen people unto thee while others shall perish.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:29} % *ALMA-31-29 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-29}

			Yea, and they say that thou hast made it known unto them
			that there shall be no Christ.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:30} % *ALMA-31-30 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-30}

			O Lord God, how long wilt thou suffer
			that such wickedness and infidelity shall be among this people?
			O Lord, wilt thou give me strength that I may bear with mine infirmities!
			For I am infirm, and such wickedness among this people doth pain my soul.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:31} % *ALMA-31-31 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-31}

			O Lord, my heart is exceeding sorrowful.
			Wilt thou comfort my soul in Christ!
			O Lord, wilt thou grant unto me that I may have strength
			that I may suffer with patience these afflictions which shall come upon me
			because of the iniquity of this people!

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:32} % *ALMA-31-32 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-32}

			O Lord, wilt thou comfort my soul and give unto me success!
			And also my fellow laborers which are with me
			---yea, Ammon and Aaron and Omner,
			and also Amulek and Zeezrom, and also my two sons---
			yea, even all these wilt thou comfort, O Lord!
			Yea, wilt thou comfort their souls in Christ!

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:33} % *ALMA-31-33 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-33}

			Wilt thou grant unto them that they may have strength,
			that they may bear their afflictions which shall come upon them
			because of the iniquities of this people!

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:34} % *ALMA-31-34 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-34}

			O Lord, wilt thou grant unto us
			that we may have success in bringing them again unto thee in Christ!

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:35} % *ALMA-31-35 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-35}

			Behold, O Lord, their souls are precious;
			and many of them are our near brethren.
			Therefore give unto us, O Lord, power and wisdom
			that we may bring these our brethren again unto thee.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:36} % *ALMA-31-36 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-36}

			Now it came to pass that when Alma had said these words
			that he clapped%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Note definition \#4 in the 1828 for the transitive verb ``clap'': ``To thrust; to put, place or send; followed by in, into, under, over, etc.; as, to \textit{clap} one under the hatches; to \textit{clap} one into Bedlam; to \textit{clap} a board over a pit.''
					}
			his hands upon all they which were with him.
			And behold, as he clapped his hands upon them,
			they were filled with the Holy Spirit.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:37} % *ALMA-31-37 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-37}

			And after that, they did separate themselves one from another,
			taking no thought for themselves what they should eat
			or what they should drink or what they should put on.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 31:38} % *ALMA-31-38 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-31-38}

			And the Lord provided for them
			that they should hunger not, neither should they thirst.
			Yea, and he also gave them strength that they should suffer no manner of afflictions
			save it were swallowed up in the joy of Christ.
			Now this was according to the prayer of Alma---
			and this because he prayed in faith.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Alma 32} % *ALMA-32 
	% -----
		\label{ALMA-32}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:1} % *ALMA-32-1 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-1}

			\footnote{%
				% \label{}%
				I wrote two Rainbows essays on different aspects of this chapter. See my PDF highlights in preparation for the essays \href{https://drive.google.com/file/d/1LNlTYcxh5Q_PlKdVUidgKDDh7B2GLXJO/view?usp=drive_link}{here}; see \href{https://climbingtherainbows.substack.com/p/knowing-the-seed-truth-goodness-alma-32-p1}{part 1} of the essays, and \href{https://climbingtherainbows.substack.com/p/knowing-the-seed-truth-goodness-alma-32-p2}{part 2}.
				}%
			And it came to pass that they did go forth
			and began to preach the word of God unto the people,
			entering into their synagogues and into their houses;
			yea, and even they did preach the word in their streets.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:2} % *ALMA-32-2 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-2}

			And it came to pass that after much labor among them,
			they began to have success among the poorer class of the people;
			for behold, they were cast out of the synagogues
			because of the coarseness of their apparel.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:3} % *ALMA-32-3 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-3}

			Therefore they were not permitted
			to enter into their synagogues to worship God,
			being esteemed as filthiness.
			Therefore they were poor;
			yea, they were esteemed by their brethren as dross.
			Therefore they were poor as to things of the world;
			and also they were poor in heart.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:4} % *ALMA-32-4 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-4}

			Now as Alma was teaching and speaking unto the people upon the hill Onidah,
			there came a great multitude unto him,
			which were those of which we have been speaking,
			which were poor in heart because of their poverty as to things of the world;

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:5} % *ALMA-32-5 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-5}

			and they came unto Alma.
			And the one which was the most foremost among them saith unto him:
			Behold, what shall these my brethren do?%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Reminiscent of what the people ask in \hyperref[LUKE-3-10]{Luke 3:10}.
					}
			For they are despised of all men because of their poverty,
			yea, and more especially by our priests.
			For they have cast us out of our synagogues,
			which we have labored abundantly to build with our own hands.
			And they have cast us out because of this our exceeding poverty,
			that we have no place to worship our God.
			And now behold, what shall we do?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:6} % *ALMA-32-6 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-6}

			And now when Alma heard this,
			he turned him about, his face immediately towards him.
			And he beheld with great joy,
			for he beheld that their afflictions had truly humbled them
			and that they were in a preparation to hear the word.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:7} % *ALMA-32-7 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-7}

			Therefore he did say no more to the other multitude,
			but he stretched forth his hand
			and cried unto those which he beheld, which were truly penitent,
			and saith unto them:

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:8} % *ALMA-32-8 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-8}

			I behold that ye are lowly in heart;
			and if so, blessed are ye.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:9} % *ALMA-32-9 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-9}

			Behold, thy brother hath said:
			What shall we do?
			For we are cast out of our synagogues that we cannot worship our God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:10} % *ALMA-32-10 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-10}

			Behold, I say unto you:
			Do ye suppose that ye cannot worship God save it be in your synagogues only?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:11} % *ALMA-32-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-11}

			And moreover, I would ask:
			Do ye suppose that ye must not worship God, only once in a week?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:12} % *ALMA-32-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-12}

			I say unto you:
			It is well that ye are cast out of your synagogues,
			that ye may be humble and that ye may learn wisdom;
			for it is necessary that ye should learn wisdom.
			For it is because that ye are cast out
			that ye are despised of your brethren because of your exceeding poverty,
			that ye are brought to a lowliness of heart;
			for ye are necessarily brought to be humble.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:13} % *ALMA-32-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-13}

			And now because ye are compelled to be humble, blessed are ye;
			for a man sometimes, if he is compelled to be humble, seeketh repentance.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Compare \hyperref[DC56-17]{DC 56:17--18}.
					}
			And now surely, whosoever repenteth shall find mercy.
			And he that findeth mercy and endureth to the end,
			the same shall be saved.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:14} % *ALMA-32-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-14}

			And now as I said unto you
			that because ye were compelled to be humble ye were blessed,
			do ye not suppose that they are more blessed
			who truly humble%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					``Truly humble'' occurs here and in the next verse; see also \hyperref[DC97-1]{DC 97:1} and more on that phrase \hyperref[HUMILITY-PHRASES]{here}.
					}
			themselves because of the word?%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For the distinction between being compelled to be humble and humbling yourself, see \hyperref[HUMILITY-yourself]{this study}.
					}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:15} % *ALMA-32-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-15}

			Yea, he that truly humbleth himself
			and repenteth of his sins and endureth to the end,
			the same shall be blessed---
			yea, much more blessed than they who art compelled to be humble
			because of their exceeding poverty.

		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 32:16} % *ALMA-32-16
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-16}
			
			Therefore blessed are they who humbleth themselves
			without being compelled%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[LUKE-14-23]{Luke 14:23}, \hyperref[ALMA-62-41]{Alma 62:41},
				}
			to be humble.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Compare \hyperref[MATTHEW-23-12]{Matthew 23:12}.
					}
			Or rather, in other words, blessed is he
			that believeth in the word of God and is baptized%
				\footnote{%
					\label{ALMA-32-16-baptized}%
					I think you can understand the ``baptism'' or ``being baptized'' here as whatever we need to do next to repent, or whatever the Spirit is telling us to do. Sometimes those things can be very hard. Compare the use of ``baptism'' in \hyperref[MARK-10-38]{Mark 10:38} and \hyperref[LUKE-12-50]{Luke 12:50}. See also \hyperref[LUKE-7-29]{Luke 7:29--30}, \hyperref[ROMANS-2-13]{Romans 2:13}, \hyperref[ROMANS-2-17]{Romans 2:17--18}, \hyperref[DC55-1]{DC 55:1}, 
					}
			without stubbornness of heart,
			yea, without being brought to know the word---or even compelled%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					One very important connection here is \hyperref[DC58-26]{DC 58:26--33}, but see also \hyperref[1NEPHI-18-20]{1 Nephi 18:20}, \hyperref[ALMA-60-32]{Alma 60:32--33},
					}
			to know---
			before they will believe.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:17} % *ALMA-32-17 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-17}

			Yea, there are many which do say:
			If thou wilt show unto us a sign from heaven,
			then we shall know of a surety;
			then we shall believe.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:18} % *ALMA-32-18 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-18}

			Now I ask:
			Is this faith?
			Behold, I say unto you: Nay.
			For if a man knoweth a thing, he hath no cause to believe,
			for he knoweth it.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:19} % *ALMA-32-19 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-19}

			And now, how much more cursed is he
			that knoweth the will of God and doeth it not%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[LUKE-12-47]{Luke 12:47--48}; \hyperref[EPHESIANS-1-9]{Ephesians 1:9}; \hyperref[JAMES-4-17]{James 4:17}; \hyperref[MOSIAH-3-11]{Mosiah 3:11} (this is one of the most important ones); \hyperref[3NEPHI-6-18]{3 Nephi 6:18} (also important); \hyperref[DC58-1]{DC 58:1}; \hyperref[DC63-1]{DC 63:1}, \hyperref[DC63-20]{20}, \hyperref[DC63-22]{22}, \hyperref[DC63-24]{24}, and \hyperref[DC63-46]{46}; \hyperref[DC64-1]{DC 64:1}; \hyperref[DC66-4]{DC 66:4}; \hyperref[DC75-3]{DC 75:3}; \hyperref[DC80]{DC 80}  (especially verse 5); \hyperref[DC82-8]{DC 82:8}; \hyperref[DC88-1]{DC 88:1}; \hyperref[DC103-31]{DC 103:31}; \hyperref[DC109-68]{DC 109:68} (there are others in the DC too); JS on \hyperref[EMS-1842-JS-18-AUG-1842]{18 August 1842} (``I know the goodness of your hearts, and that you will do the will of the Lord, when it is made known to you''); 
					}
			than he that only believeth, or only hath cause to believe,
			and falleth into transgression?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:20} % *ALMA-32-20 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-20}

			Now of this thing ye must judge.
			Behold, I say unto you that it is on the one hand even as it is on the other;
			and it shall be unto every man according to his work.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:21} % *ALMA-32-21 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-21}

			And now as I said concerning faith:
			faith is not to have a perfect knowledge of things;
			therefore if ye have faith,
			\footnote{%
				% \label{}%
				This ``ye hope...'' part looks related to \hyperref[ROMANS-8-24]{Romans 8:24--25}.
				}%
			ye hope for things which is not seen,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[2CORINTHIANS-4-18]{2 Corinthians 4:18},
					}
			which are true.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:22} % *ALMA-32-22 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-22}

			And now behold, I say unto you---and I would that ye should remember---
			that God is merciful unto all who believe on his name.
			Therefore he desireth in the first place
			that ye should believe, yea, even on his word.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:23} % *ALMA-32-23 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-23}

			And now he imparteth his word by angels unto men,
			yea, not only men but women also.
			Now this is not all.
			Little children doth have words given unto them many times
			which doth confound the wise and the learned.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:24} % *ALMA-32-24 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-24}

			And now my beloved brethren, as ye have desired to know of me
			what ye shall do because ye are a‹icted and cast out
			---now I do not desire that ye should suppose that I mean to judge you,
			only according to that which is true---

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:25} % *ALMA-32-25 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-25}

			for I do not mean that ye, all of you, have been compelled to humble yourselves.
			For I verily believe there are some among you which would humble themselves,
			let him be in whatsoever circumstances he might.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:26} % *ALMA-32-26 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-26}

			Now as I said concerning faith, that it was not a perfect knowledge,
			even so it is with my words.
			Ye cannot know of their surety at first unto perfection,
			any more than faith is a perfect knowledge.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:27} % *ALMA-32-27 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-27}

			But behold, if ye will awake and arouse your faculties,
			even to an experiment upon my words,
			and exercise a particle of faith
			---yea, even if ye can no more than desire to believe---
			let this desire work in you, even until ye believe in a manner
			that ye can give place for a portion of my words.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:28} % *ALMA-32-28 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-28}

				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Think of these passages as a commentary on the parable of the sower at the beginning of \hyperref[MATTHEW-13]{Matthew 13}.
					}%
			Now we will compare the word unto a seed.
			Now if ye give place that a seed may be planted in your heart,
			behold, if it be a true seed or a good seed,
			if ye do not cast it out by your unbelief
			---that ye will resist the Spirit of the Lord---
			behold, it will begin to swell within your breasts.
			And when you feel these swelling motions,
			ye will begin to say within yourselves:
			It must needs be that this is a good seed, or that the word is good,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					We make this judgment on the basis of something we personally experience and feel. See \hyperref[JOHN-6-26]{John 6:26--27}, 
					}%
			for it beginneth to enlarge my soul.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC121-42]{DC 121:42}.
					}
			Yea, it beginneth to enlighten my understanding;
			yea, and it beginneth to be delicious to me.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:29} % *ALMA-32-29 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-29}

			Now behold, would not this increase your faith?
			I say unto you: Yea.
			Nevertheless it hath not grown up to a perfect knowledge.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:30} % *ALMA-32-30 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-30}

			But behold, as the seed swelleth and sprouteth and beginneth to grow
			and then ye must needs say that the seed is good,
			for behold, it swelleth and sprouteth and beginneth to grow.
			And now behold, will not this strengthen your faith?
			Yea, it will strengthen your faith.
			For ye will say:
			I know that this is a good seed,
			for behold, it sprouteth and beginneth to grow.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:31} % *ALMA-32-31 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-31}

			And now behold, are ye sure that this is a good seed?
			I say unto you: Yea.
			For every seed bringeth forth unto its own likeness.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:32} % *ALMA-32-32 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-32}

			Therefore if a seed groweth, it is good.
			But if it groweth not, behold, it is not good;
			therefore it is cast away.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:33} % *ALMA-32-33 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-33}

			And now behold, because ye have tried the experiment and planted the seed
			and it swelleth and sprouteth and beginneth to grow,
			ye must needs know that the seed is good.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:34} % *ALMA-32-34 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-34}

			And now behold, is your knowledge perfect?
			Yea, your knowledge is perfect in that thing and your faith is dormant---
			and this because you know.
			For ye know that the word hath swelled your souls.
			And ye also know that it hath sprouted up,
			that your understanding doth begin to be enlightened
			and your mind doth begin to expand.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					In a \hyperref[EMS-1840-JS-14-MAY-1840]{14 May 1840} letter, JS wrote the following to Orson Hyde and John E. Page: ``therefore, inasmuch as you are to be instrumental in this great work, he will endow you with power, wisdom, might, and inteligence; and every qualification necessary; while your minds will expand wider and wider, untill you can circumscribe the Earth, \& the Heavens, and reach forth into eternity; contemplate the mighty acts of Jehovah, in all their variety \& glory.''
					}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:35} % *ALMA-32-35 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-35}

			O then is not this real?
			I say unto you:
			Yea, because it is light.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Compare \hyperref[MOSIAH-8-17]{Mosiah 8:17}, \hyperref[DC50-24]{DC 50:24}, \hyperref[DC84-45]{DC 84:45}; but especially \hyperref[MOSES-1-15]{Moses 1:15}---there, the ``darkness'' is not the quickening, vitalizing power that we experience and use as the basis for the judgment.
					}
			And
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For this ``whatsoever is light'' bit, see also \hyperref[EPHESIANS-5-13]{Ephesians 5:13}.
					}%
			whatsoever is light is good
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Perhaps, ``whatsoever is light is good, [and we can tell it is good] because [the goodness] is discernible; therefore ye must know that it is good.''
					}%
			because it is
			discernible;%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For commentary on ``discernible,'' see \hyperref[EXSS-BOM-ALMA-32-DISCERNIBLE]{this study}.
					}
			therefore ye must know that it is good.
			And now behold, after ye have tasted this light, is your knowledge perfect?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:36} % *ALMA-32-36 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-36}

			Behold, I say unto you: Nay.
			Neither must ye lay aside your faith,
			for ye have only exercised your faith to plant the seed
			that ye might try the experiment to know if the seed was good.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:37} % *ALMA-32-37 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-37}

			And behold, as the tree beginneth to grow, ye will say:
			Let us nourish it with great care that it may get root,
			that it may grow up and bring forth fruit unto us.
			And now behold, if ye nourish it with much care,
			it will get root and grow up and bring forth fruit.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:38} % *ALMA-32-38 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-38}

			But if ye neglect the tree and take no thought for its nourishment,
			behold, it will not get any root.
			And when the heat of the sun cometh and scorcheth it,
			and because it hath no root, it withereth away;
			and ye pluck it up and cast it out.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:39} % *ALMA-32-39 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-39}

			Now this is not because the seed was not good,
			neither is it because the fruit thereof would not be desirable.
			But it is because your ground is barren and ye will not nourish the tree;
			therefore ye cannot have the fruit thereof.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:40} % *ALMA-32-40 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-40}

			And thus it is: if ye will not nourish the word,
			looking forward with an eye of faith to the fruit thereof,
			ye can never pluck of the fruit of the tree of life.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:41} % *ALMA-32-41 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-41}

			But if ye will nourish the word,
			yea, nourish the tree as it beginneth to grow by your faith
			with great diligence and with patience,
			looking forward to the fruit thereof,
			and it shall take root.
			And behold, it shall be a tree springing up unto everlasting life.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:42} % *ALMA-32-42 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-42}

			And because of your diligence and your faith and your patience with the word
			in nourishing it that it may take root in you,
			behold, by and by ye shall pluck the fruit thereof, which is most precious,
			which is sweet above all that is sweet,
			and which is white above all that is white,
			yea, and pure above all that is pure;
			and ye shall feast upon this fruit even until ye are filled,
			that ye hunger not, neither shall ye thirst.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 32:43} % *ALMA-32-43 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-32-43}

			Then, my brethren, ye shall reap the rewards of your faith
			and your diligence and patience and long-suffering,
			waiting for the tree to bring forth fruit unto you.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Alma 33} % *ALMA-33 
	% -----
		\label{ALMA-33}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:1} % *ALMA-33-1 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-1}

			Now after Alma had spoken these words,
			they sent forth unto him, desiring to know
			whether they should believe in one God,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The fact that they ask this question suggests that some people among them, or at least people they knew about, were polytheists, and that the people listening to Alma were wondering about those beliefs.
					}
			that they might obtain this fruit of which he had spoken;
			or how they should plant the seed, or the word of which he had spoken,
			which he said must be planted in their hearts;
			or in what manner they should begin to exercise their faith.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The people ask three questions about what Alma has said so far:
						\begin{compactitem}
							\item In order to get the fruit, should we believe in one God?
							\item How do we plant the seed, or the word that you said we had to plant in our hearts?
							\item How do we begin to exercise our faith?
						\end{compactitem}
					}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:2} % *ALMA-33-2 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-2}

			And Alma saith unto them:
			Behold, ye have said that ye could not worship your God
			because ye are cast out of your synagogues.
			But behold, I say unto you:
			If ye suppose that ye cannot worship your God, ye do greatly err,
			and ye had ought to search the scriptures;
			for if ye suppose that they have taught you this,
			ye do not understand them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:3} % *ALMA-33-3 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-3}

			Do ye remember to have read
			what Zenos the prophet of old hath said concerning prayer or worship?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:4} % *ALMA-33-4 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-4}

			For he saith:
			Thou art merciful, O God,
			for thou hast heard my prayer,
			even when I was in the wilderness.
			Yea, thou wast merciful
			when I prayed concerning those which were mine enemies,
			and thou didst turn them to me.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:5} % *ALMA-33-5 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-5}

			Yea, O God, and thou wast merciful unto me
			when I did cry unto thee in my field,
			when I did cry unto thee in my prayer,
			and thou didst hear me.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:6} % *ALMA-33-6 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-6}

			And again, O God, when I did turn to my house,
			thou didst hear me in my prayer.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:7} % *ALMA-33-7 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-7}

			And when I did turn unto my closet, O Lord,
			and prayed unto thee, thou didst hear me.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:8} % *ALMA-33-8 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-8}

			Yea, thou art merciful unto thy children;
			when they cry unto thee to be heard of thee and not of men,
			and thou wilt hear them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:9} % *ALMA-33-9 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-9}

			Yea, O God, thou hast been merciful unto me
			and heard my cries in the midst of thy congregations.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:10} % *ALMA-33-10 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-10}

			Yea, and thou hast also heard me
			when I have been cast out and have been despised by mine enemies.
			Yea, thou didst hear my cries and wast angry with mine enemies,
			and thou didst visit them in thine anger with speedy destruction.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:11} % *ALMA-33-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-11}

			And thou didst hear me because of mine afflictions and my sincerity.
			And it is because of thy Son that thou hast been thus merciful unto me.
			Therefore I will cry unto thee in all mine afflictions,
			for in thee is my joy;
			for thou hast turned thy judgments away from me because of thy Son.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:12} % *ALMA-33-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-12}

			And now Alma saith unto them:
			Do ye believe those scriptures which have been written by them of old?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:13} % *ALMA-33-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-13}

			Behold, if ye do, ye must believe what Zenos saith,
			for behold, he saith:
			Thou hast turned away thy judgments because of thy Son.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:14} % *ALMA-33-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-14}

			Now behold, my brethren, I would ask if ye have read these scriptures?
			If ye have, how can ye disbelieve on the Son of God?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:15} % *ALMA-33-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-15}

			For it is not written that Zenos alone spake of these things,
			but Zenoch also spake of these things,

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:16} % *ALMA-33-16 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-16}

			for behold, he saith:
			Thou art angry, O Lord, with this people
			because they will not understand of thy mercies
			which thou hast bestowed upon them because of thy Son.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:17} % *ALMA-33-17 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-17}

			And now my brethren, ye see
			that a second prophet of old has testified of the Son of God.
			And because the people would not understand his words,
			they stoned him to death.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:18} % *ALMA-33-18 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-18}

			But behold, this is not all.
			These are not the only ones which have spoken concerning the Son of God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:19} % *ALMA-33-19 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-19}

			Behold, he was spoken of by Moses.
			Yea, and behold, a type was raised up in the wilderness,
			that whosoever would look upon it might live;
			and many did look and live.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:20} % *ALMA-33-20 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-20}

			But few understood the meaning of those things---
			and this because of the hardness of their hearts.
			But there were many which were so hardened that they would not look;
			therefore they perished.
			Now the reason that they would not look
			is because they did not believe that it would heal them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:21} % *ALMA-33-21 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-21}

			O my brethren, if ye could be healed
			by merely casting about your eyes that ye might behold,
			would ye not behold quickly?
			Or would ye rather harden your hearts in unbelief and be slothful,
			that ye would not cast about your eyes, that ye might perish?

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:22} % *ALMA-33-22 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-22}

			If so, woe shall come upon you.
			But if not so, then cast about your eyes
			and begin to believe in the Son of God,
			that he will come to redeem his people
			and that he shall suffer and die to atone for their sins
			and that he shall rise again from the dead,
			which shall bring to pass the resurrection,
			that all men shall stand before him
			to be judged at the last and judgment day according to their works.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 33:23} % *ALMA-33-23 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-33-23}

			And now my brethren, I desire that ye should plant this word in your hearts.
			And as it beginneth to swell, even so nourish it by your faith.
			And behold, it will become a tree springing up in you unto everlasting life.
			And then may God grant unto you
			that your burdens may be light through the joy of his Son.
			And even all this can ye do if ye will.
			Amen.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Alma 34} % *ALMA-34 
	% -----
		\label{ALMA-34}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:1} % *ALMA-34-1 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-1}

			And now it came to pass that
			after Alma had spoken these words unto them,
			he sat down upon the ground.
			And Amulek arose and began to teach them, saying:

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:2} % *ALMA-34-2 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-2}

			My brethren, I think that it is impossible
			that ye should be ignorant of the things
			which have been spoken concerning the coming of Christ,
			who is taught by us to be the Son of God.
			Yea, I know that these things were taught unto you bountifully
			before your dissension from among us.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:3} % *ALMA-34-3 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-3}

			And as ye have desired of my beloved brother
			that he should make known unto you
			what ye should do because of your afflictions---
			and he hath spoken somewhat unto you to prepare your minds,
			yea, and he hath exhorted you unto faith and to patience,

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:4} % *ALMA-34-4 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-4}

			yea, even that ye would have so much faith
			as even to plant the word in your heart,
			that ye may try the experiment of its goodness.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:5} % *ALMA-34-5 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-5}

			And we have beheld that the great question which is in your minds
			is whether the word be in the Son of God or whether there shall be no Christ.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:6} % *ALMA-34-6 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-6}

			And ye also behold that my brother hath proven unto you in many instances
			that the word is in Christ unto salvation.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:7} % *ALMA-34-7 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-7}

			My brother hath called upon the words of Zenos,
			that redemption cometh through the Son of God,
			and also upon the words of Zenoch.
			And also he hath appealed unto Moses to prove that these things are true.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:8} % *ALMA-34-8 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-8}

			And now behold, I will testify unto you of myself that these things are true.
			Behold, I say unto you that I do know
			that Christ shall come among the children of men
			to take upon him the transgressions of his people
			and that he shall atone for the sins of the world,
			for the Lord God hath spoken it.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:9} % *ALMA-34-9 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-9}

			For it is expedient that an atonement should be made,
			for according to the great plans of the Eternal God
			there must be an atonement made or else all mankind must unavoidably perish.
			Yea, all are hardened;
			yea, all are fallen and are lost and must perish
			except it be through the atonement, which it is expedient should be made.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:10} % *ALMA-34-10 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-10}

			For it is expedient that there should be a great and last sacrifice

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:11} % *ALMA-34-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-11}

			Now there is not any man that can sacrifice his own blood
			which will atone for the sins of another.
			Now if a man murdereth,
			behold, will our law, which is just, take the life of his brother?
			I say unto you: Nay.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:12} % *ALMA-34-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-12}

			But the law requireth the life of him who hath murdered.
			Therefore there can be nothing which is short of an infinite atonement
			which will suffice for the sins of the world.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:13} % *ALMA-34-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-13}

			Therefore it is expedient that there should be a great and last sacrifice.
			And then shall there be---or it is expedient there should be---
			a stop to the shedding of blood;
			then shall the law of Moses be fulfilled.
			Yea, it shall all be fulfilled, every jot and tittle,
			and none shall have passed away.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:14} % *ALMA-34-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-14}

			And behold, this is the whole meaning of the law,
			every whit a pointing to that great and last sacrifice;
			and that great and last sacrifice will be the Son of God,
			yea, infinite and eternal.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:15} % *ALMA-34-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-15}

			And thus he shall bring salvation to all those who shall believe on his name,
			this being the intent of this last sacrifice,
			to bring about the bowels of mercy, which overpowereth justice
			and bringeth about means unto men that they may have faith unto repentance.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:16} % *ALMA-34-16 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-16}

			And thus mercy can satisfy the demands of justice
			and encircle them in the arms of safety,
			while he that exerciseth no faith unto repentance
			is exposed to the whole law of the demands of justice.
			Therefore only unto him that hath faith unto repentance
			is brought about the great and eternal plan of redemption.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:17} % *ALMA-34-17 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-17}

			Therefore may God grant unto you, my brethren,
			that ye might begin to exercise your faith unto repentance,
			that ye begin to call upon his holy name
			that he would have mercy upon you.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:18} % *ALMA-34-18 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-18}

			Yea, cry unto him for mercy, for he is mighty to save.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:19} % *ALMA-34-19 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-19}

			Yea, humble yourselves and continue in prayer unto him.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:20} % *ALMA-34-20 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-20}

			Cry unto him when ye are in your fields,
			yea, over all your flocks.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:21} % *ALMA-34-21 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-21}

			Cry unto him in your houses, yea, over all your household,
			both morning, midday, and evening.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:22} % *ALMA-34-22 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-22}

			Yea, cry unto him against the power of your enemies.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:23} % *ALMA-34-23 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-23}

			Yea, cry unto him against the devil, which is an enemy to all righteousness.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:24} % *ALMA-34-24 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-24}

			Cry unto him over the crops of your fields, that ye may prosper in them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:25} % *ALMA-34-25 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-25}

			Cry over the flocks of your fields, that they may increase.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:26} % *ALMA-34-26 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-26}

			But this is not all.
			Ye must pour out your souls in your closets and your secret places
			and in your wilderness.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:27} % *ALMA-34-27 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-27}

			Yea, and when you do not cry unto the Lord,
			let your hearts be full, drawn out in prayer unto him%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					What does it mean to have our hearts ``drawn out in prayer unto the Lord''? From the OED we find the following definitions under ``draw out'':
						\begin{compactitem}
							\item[1.a] To pull or take (something) out; to extract, remove.
							\item[3.a] To pull out (something) to a greater length or size; to stretch, elongate; to flatten out (metal).
							\item[3.b] To extend the duration of (something); to protract, prolong.
							\item[4.b] To lead out (one's troops) in preparation for battle or some purpose; to detach (troops) from the main force for a particular purpose; to draw up (troops) in formation.
							\item[5] To call (something) forth; to elicit; to make a person divulge (something). Frequently with from, out of.
							\item[7.a] To persuade or entice (a person or animal) to come out into the open or leave a place of safety or comfort; to lure (a person or animal) out.
							\item[9] To elicit information from (a person); to induce (a person) to talk without restraint or reserve.
								\begin{compactitem}
									\item For this one it notes that it is ``colloquial,'' but it traces to at least 1778.
								\end{compactitem}
							\item[11] To pick out and draw attention to (something); to highlight, tease out.
								\begin{compactitem}
									\item Traces to at least 1855, so not that early.
								\end{compactitem}
						\end{compactitem} 
					I think I'm most interested here in \#1.a, \#3.a, \#5, \#7.a, and \#9. All give pretty interesting readings of this verse and deepen how I view what this passage is trying to tell us---this ``drawing out'' in prayer is part of our spiritual growth as we leave our places of comfort, share openly with the Lord, and prepare ourselves to stretch spiritually in the actions we take. Compare \hyperref[HELAMAN-13-22]{Helaman 13:22}, where the idea seems to have to do with gratitude and one's view on one's possessions (and how those possessions affect the way you relate to others).
					}
			continually for your welfare
			and also for the welfare of those which are around you.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:28} % *ALMA-34-28 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-28}

			And now behold, my brethren, I say unto you:
			Do not suppose that this is all.
			For after ye have done all these things,
			if ye turn away the needy and the naked
			and visit not the sick and afflicted
			and impart of your substance, if ye have,
			to those which stand in need---
			I say unto you,
			if ye do not any of these things,
			behold, your prayer is vain and availeth you nothing,
			and ye are as hypocrites which do deny the faith.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:29} % *ALMA-34-29 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-29}

			Therefore if ye do not remember to be charitable,
			ye are as dross which the refiners do cast out
			---it being of no worth---
			and is trodden under foot of men.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:30} % *ALMA-34-30 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-30}

			And now my brethren, I would that after ye have received so many witnesses,
			seeing that the holy scriptures testifies of these things,
			come forth and bring fruit unto repentance.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:31} % *ALMA-34-31 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-31}

			Yea, I would that ye would come forth and harden not your hearts any longer.
			For behold,
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					It looks like the ``now...day'' stuff is coming from \hyperref[2CORINTHIANS-6-2]{2 Corinthians 6:2}, which itself is quoting \hyperref[ISAIAH-49-8]{Isaiah 49:8}.
					}%
			now is the time and the day of your salvation.
			And therefore, if ye will repent and harden not your hearts,
			\hyperref[IMMEDIATELY]{immediately} shall the great plan of redemption be brought about unto you.

		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 34:32} % *ALMA-34-32
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-32}
			
			For behold, this life is the time for men to prepare to meet God.
			Yea, behold, the day of this life is the day for men to perform their labors.
			
		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 34:33} % *ALMA-34-33
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-33}
			
			And now as I said unto you before,
			as ye have had so many witnesses,
			therefore I beseech of you
			that ye do not procrastinate the day of your repentance until the end.
			For after this day of life which is given us to prepare for eternity,
			behold, if we do not improve%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					The key to the ``improve our time'' construction is the word ``improve''; basically the idea is that we are supposed to take advantage of the time given us. Meaning \#4.b for ``improve'' in the OED is ``To make good use of or make the most of (time, a situation, etc.); to take advantage of (an opportunity or occasion) (to do something).'' We also find the following quotations: 1604, ``How can they better improue their time then for the common good of their country?''; 1677, ``Yet was their Labour well improved, and followed with good Success''; 1720, ``How doth the little busy bee Improve each shining hour!''; 1774, ``The fine Weather, we have had for 8 or 10 days past I hope has been carefully improved to get in my Hay.'' This use of the word ``improve'' corresponds to meaning \#2 in the 1828 dictionary, ``To use or employ to good purpose; to make productive; to turn to profitable account; to use for advantage; to employ for advancing interest, reputation or happiness.''
					}
			our time
			while in this life,
			then cometh the night of darkness wherein there can be no labor performed.
			
		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 34:34} % *ALMA-34-34
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-34}
			
			Ye cannot say when ye are brought to that awful crisis
			that I will repent,
			that I will return to my God.
			Nay, ye cannot say this.
			For that same spirit which doth possess your bodies
			at the time that ye go out of this life,
			that same spirit will have power to possess your body in that eternal world.
			
		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 34:35} % *ALMA-34-35
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-35}
			
			For behold, if ye have procrastinated the day of your repentance even until death,
			behold, ye have become subjected to the spirit of the devil,%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC93-25]{DC 93:25}.
					}
			and he doth seal you his.
			Therefore the Spirit of the Lord hath withdrawn from you and hath no place in you,
			and the devil hath all power over you.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					If this verse were in the NT in Greek, would the ``power'' by \gr{ἐξουσία} or \gr{δύναμις}? I think the former, but I'm not sure; see the list of references in my notes to \hyperref[DC76-30]{DC 76:30}.
					}
			And this is the final state of the wicked.
			
		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 34:36} % *ALMA-34-36
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-36}
			
			And this I know because the Lord hath said
			he dwelleth not in unholy temples,
			but in the hearts of the righteous doth he dwell.
			Yea, and he hath also said
			that the righteous should sit down in his kingdom to go no more out,
			but their garments should be made white through the blood of the Lamb.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:37} % *ALMA-34-37 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-37}

			And now my beloved brethren, I desire that ye should remember these things
			and that ye should work out your salvation with fear%
			\footnote{%
				% \label{}%
				A reference to \hyperref[PHILIPPIANS-2-12]{Philippians 2:12}. The verb there is a plural imperative of \gr{κατεργάζομαι}.
				}
			before God
			and that ye should no more deny the coming of Christ,

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:38} % *ALMA-34-38 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-38}

			that ye contend no more against the Holy Ghost,
			but that ye receive it and take upon you the name of Christ,
			that ye humble yourselves even to the dust
			and worship God in whatsoever place ye may be in,
			in spirit and in truth,
			and that ye live in thanksgiving daily
			for the many mercies and blessings which he doth bestow upon you.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:39} % *ALMA-34-39 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-39}

			Yea, and I also exhort you, my brethren,
			that ye be watchful unto prayer continually,
			that ye may not be led away by the temptations of the devil,
			that he may not overpower you,
			that ye may not become his subjects at the last day.
			For behold, he rewardeth you no good thing.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:40} % *ALMA-34-40 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-40}

			And now my beloved brethren, I would exhort you to have patience
			and that ye bear with all manner of afflictions,
			that ye do not revile against those
			who do cast you out because of your exceeding poverty,
			lest ye become sinners like unto them,

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 34:41} % *ALMA-34-41 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-34-41}

			but that ye have patience and bear with those afflictions
			with a firm hope that ye shall one day rest from all your afflictions.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Alma 35} % *ALMA-35 
	% -----
		\label{ALMA-35}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:1} % *ALMA-35-1 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-1}

			Now it came to pass that after Amulek had made an end of these words,
			they withdrew themselves from the multitude
			and came over into the land of Jershon.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:2} % *ALMA-35-2 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-2}

			Yea, and the rest of the brethren,
			after they had preached the word unto the Zoramites,
			also came over into the land of Jershon.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:3} % *ALMA-35-3 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-3}

			And it came to pass that
			after the more popular part of the Zoramites had consulted together
			concerning the words which had been preached unto them,
			they were angry because of the word,
			for it did destroy their craft;
			therefore they would not hearken unto the words.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:4} % *ALMA-35-4 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-4}

			And they sent and gathered together throughout all the land all the people
			and consulted with them concerning the words which had been spoken.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:5} % *ALMA-35-5 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-5}

			Now their rulers and their priests and their teachers
			did not let the people know concerning their desires.
			Therefore they found out privily the minds of all the people.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:6} % *ALMA-35-6 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-6}

			And it came to pass that after they had found out the minds of all the people,
			those which were in favor of the words
			which had been spoken by Alma and his brethren
			were cast out of the land---and they were many---
			and they came over also into the land of Jershon.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:7} % *ALMA-35-7 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-7}

			And it came to pass that Alma and his brethren did minister unto them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:8} % *ALMA-35-8 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-8}

			Now the people of the Zoramites were angry
			with the people of Ammon, which were in Jershon.
			And the chief ruler of the Zoramites being a very wicked man
			sent over unto the people of Ammon,
			desiring them that they should cast out of their land
			all those which came over from them into their land.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:9} % *ALMA-35-9 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-9}

			And he breathed out many threatenings against them.
			And now the people of Ammon did not fear their words.
			Therefore they did not cast them out,
			but they did receive all the poor of the Zoramites that came over unto them.
			And they did nourish them and did clothe them
			and did give unto them lands for their inheritance.
			And they did administer unto them according to their wants.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:10} % *ALMA-35-10 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-10}

			Now this did stir up the Zoramites to anger against the people of Ammon.
			And they began to mix with the Lamanites
			and to stir them up also to anger against them.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:11} % *ALMA-35-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-11}

			And thus the Zoramites and the Lamanites began to make preparations for war
			against the people of Ammon and also against the Nephites.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:12} % *ALMA-35-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-12}

			And thus ended the seventeenth year
			of the reign of the judges over the people of Nephi.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:13} % *ALMA-35-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-13}

			And the people of Ammon departed out of the land of Jershon
			and came over into the land of Melek
			and gave place in the land of Jershon for the armies of the Nephites,
			that they might contend with the armies of the Lamanites
			and the armies of the Zoramites.
			And thus commenced a war betwixt the Lamanites and the Nephites
			in the eighteenth year of the reign of the judges.
			And an account shall be given of their wars hereafter.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:14} % *ALMA-35-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-14}

			And Alma and Ammon and their brethren and also the two sons of Alma
			returned to the land of Zarahemla,
			after having been instruments in the hands of God
			of bringing many of the Zoramites to repentance.
			And as many as were brought to repentance were driven out of their land;
			but they have lands for their inheritance in the land of Jershon.
			And they have taken up arms to defend themselves
			and their wives and their children and their lands.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:15} % *ALMA-35-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-15}

			Now Alma being grieved for the iniquity of his people,
			yea, for the wars and the bloodsheds and the contentions which were among them,
			and having been to declare the word---or sent to declare the word---
			among all the people in every city,
			and seeing that the hearts of the people began to wax hard
			and that they began to be \hyperref[OFFENSE]{offended} because of the strictness of the word,
			his heart was exceeding sorrowful.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 35:16} % *ALMA-35-16 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-35-16}

			Therefore he caused that his sons should be gathered together
			that he might give unto them every one his charge separately
			concerning the things pertaining unto righteousness.
			And we have an account of his commandments which he gave unto them
			according to his own record.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Alma 36} % *ALMA-36 
	% -----
		\label{ALMA-36}
		
		The commandments of Alma to his son Helaman.
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:1} % *ALMA-36-1 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-1}

			My son, give ear to my words,
			for I swear unto you
			that inasmuch as ye shall keep the commandments of God,
			ye shall prosper in the land.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:2} % *ALMA-36-2 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-2}

			I would that ye should do as I have done
			in remembering the captivity of our fathers,
			for they were in bondage and none could deliver them
			except it were the God of Abraham and the God of Isaac and the God of Jacob;
			and he surely did deliver them in their afflictions.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:3} % *ALMA-36-3 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-3}

			And now, O my son Helaman,
			behold, thou art in thy youth,
			and therefore I beseech of thee
			that thou wilt hear my words and learn of me,
			for I do know that whomsoever shall put his trust in God
			shall be supported in their trials and their troubles and their afflictions
			and shall be lifted up at the last day.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:4} % *ALMA-36-4 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-4}

			And I would not that ye think that I know of myself---
			not of the temporal but of the spiritual,
			not of the carnal mind but of God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:5} % *ALMA-36-5 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-5}

			Now behold, I say unto you:
			If I had not been born of God, I should not have known these things.
			But God hath by the mouth of his holy angel made these things known unto me,
			not of any worthiness of myself.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:6} % *ALMA-36-6 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-6}

			For I went about with the sons of Mosiah seeking to destroy the church of God.
			But behold, God sent his holy angel to stop us by the way.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:7} % *ALMA-36-7 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-7}

			And behold, he spake unto us as it were the voice of thunder,
			and the whole earth did tremble beneath our feet.
			And we all fell to the earth,
			for the fear of the Lord came upon us.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:8} % *ALMA-36-8 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-8}

			But behold, the voice said unto me: Arise.
			And I arose and stood up and beheld the angel.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:9} % *ALMA-36-9 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-9}

			And he said unto me:
			If thou wilt of thyself be destroyed,
			seek no more to destroy the church of God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:10} % *ALMA-36-10 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-10}

			And it came to pass that I fell to the earth;
			and it was for the space of three days and three nights
			that I could not open my mouth,
			neither had I the use of my limbs.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:11} % *ALMA-36-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-11}

			And the angel spake more things unto me,
			which were heard by my brethren,
			but I did not hear them.
			For when I heard the words,
			if thou wilt be destroyed of thyself,
			seek no more to destroy the church of God,
			I was struck with such great fear and amazement
			lest perhaps that I should be destroyed
			that I fell to the earth and I did hear no more.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:12} % *ALMA-36-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-12}

			But I was racked with eternal torment,
			for my soul was harrowed up to the greatest degree
			and racked with all my sins.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:13} % *ALMA-36-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-13}

			Yea, I did remember all my sins and iniquities,
			for which I was tormented with the pains of hell.
			Yea, I saw that I had rebelled against my God
			and that I had not kept his holy commandments.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:14} % *ALMA-36-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-14}

			Yea, and I had murdered many of his children
			---or rather led them away unto destruction---
			yea, and in fine, so great had been my iniquities
			that the very thoughts of coming into the presence of my God
			did rack my soul with inexpressible horror.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:15} % *ALMA-36-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-15}

			O, thought I, that I could be banished and become extinct, both soul and body,
			that I might not be brought to stand in the presence of my God
			to be judged of my deeds.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:16} % *ALMA-36-16 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-16}

			And now for three days and for three nights was I racked,
			even with the pains of a damned soul.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:17} % *ALMA-36-17 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-17}

			And it came to pass that as I was thus racked with torment,
			while I was harrowed up by the memory of my many sins,
			behold, I remembered also to have heard my father prophesy unto the people
			concerning the coming of one Jesus Christ, a Son of God,
			to atone for the sins of the world.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:18} % *ALMA-36-18 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-18}

			Now as my mind catched hold upon this thought,
			I cried within my heart:
			O Jesus, thou Son of God, have mercy on me,
			who art in the \hyperref[GALL]{gall} of bitterness
			and art encircled about by the everlasting chains of death.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:19} % *ALMA-36-19 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-19}

			And now behold, when I thought this,
			I could remember my pains no more.
			Yea, I was harrowed up by the memory of my sins no more.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:20} % *ALMA-36-20 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-20}

			And O what joy and what marvelous light I did behold!
			Yea, my soul was filled with joy as exceeding as was my pains.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:21} % *ALMA-36-21 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-21}

			Yea, I say unto you, my son,
			that there can be nothing so exquisite%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For ``exquisite'' here and later in the verse, see \hyperref[DC19-15]{DC 19:15} and notes there.
					}
			and so bitter as was my pains.
			Yea, and again I say unto you, my son,
			that on the other hand
			there can be nothing so exquisite and sweet as was my joy.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:22} % *ALMA-36-22 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-22}

			Yea, and methought I saw, even as our father Lehi saw,
			God sitting upon his throne,
			surrounded with numberless concourses of angels
			in the attitude of singing and praising their God.
			Yea, and my soul did long to be there.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:23} % *ALMA-36-23 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-23}

			But behold, my limbs did receive their strength again,
			and I stood upon my feet and did manifest unto the people
			that I had been born of God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:24} % *ALMA-36-24 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-24}

			Yea, and from that time even until now I have labored without ceasing
			that I might bring souls unto repentance,
			that I might bring them to taste of the exceeding joy of which I did taste,
			that they might also be born of God and be filled with the Holy Ghost.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:25} % *ALMA-36-25 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-25}

			Yea, and now behold, O my son,
			the Lord doth give me exceeding great joy in the fruits of my labors;

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:26} % *ALMA-36-26 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-26}

			for because of the word which he hath imparted unto me,
			behold, many hath been born of God
			and hath tasted as I have tasted
			and hath seen eye to eye as I have seen.
			Therefore they do know of these things of which I have spoken as I do know;
			and the knowledge which I have is of God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:27} % *ALMA-36-27 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-27}

			And I have been supported under trials and troubles of every kind,
			yea, and in all manner of afflictions.
			Yea, God hath delivered me from prisons and from bonds and from death.
			Yea, and I do put my trust in him and he will still deliver me.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:28} % *ALMA-36-28 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-28}

			And I know that he will raise me up at the last day to dwell with him in glory.
			Yea, and I will praise him forever.
			For he hath brought our fathers out of Egypt,
			and he hath swallowed up the Egyptians in the Red Sea,
			and he led them by his power into the promised land;
			yea, and he hath delivered them out of bondage and captivity from time to time.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:29} % *ALMA-36-29 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-29}

			Yea, and he hath also brought our fathers out of the land of Jerusalem,
			and he hath also by his everlasting power delivered them
			out of bondage and captivity from time to time, even down to the present day.
			And I have always retained in remembrance their captivity;
			yea, and ye also had ought to retain in remembrance, as I have done, their captivity.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 36:30} % *ALMA-36-30 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-36-30}

			But behold, my son, this is not all.
			For ye had ought to know as I do know
			that inasmuch as ye shall keep the commandments of God
			ye shall prosper in the land;
			and ye had ought to know also
			that inasmuch as ye will not keep the commandments of God,
			ye shall be cut off from his presence.
			Now this is according to his word.

	% -----
	\subsubsection{Alma 37} % *ALMA-37 
	% -----
		\label{ALMA-37}
		
		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:1} % *ALMA-37-1 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-1}

			And now my son Helaman,
			I command you that ye take the records
			which have been entrusted with me.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:2} % *ALMA-37-2 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-2}

			And I also command you
			that ye shall keep a record of this people,
			according as I have done,
			upon the plates of Nephi
			and keep all these things sacred which I have kept,
			even as I have kept them
			---for it is for a wise purpose that they are kept---

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:3} % *ALMA-37-3 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-3}

			and these plates of brass, which contain these engravings,
			which have the records of the holy scriptures upon them,
			which have the genealogy of our forefathers, even from the beginning.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:4} % *ALMA-37-4 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-4}

			And behold, it hath been prophesied by our fathers
			that they should be kept and handed down from one generation to another,
			and be kept and preserved by the hand of the Lord
			until they should go forth unto every nation, kindred, tongue, and people,
			that they shall know of the mysteries contained thereon.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:5} % *ALMA-37-5 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-5}

			And now behold, if they are kept, they must retain their
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					Compare \hyperref[2NEPHI-9-44]{2 Nephi 9:44}.
					}%
			brightness.
			Yea, and they will retain their brightness---
			yea, and also shall all the plates which do contain that which is holy writ.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:6} % *ALMA-37-6 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-6}

			Now ye may suppose that this is foolishness in me.
			But behold, I say unto you
			that by small and simple things are great things brought to pass.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					See \hyperref[DC64-33]{DC 64:33}.
					}
			And small means in many instances doth confound the wise.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:7} % *ALMA-37-7 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-7}

			And the Lord God doth work by means%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					I take this to mean that the Lord works by \textit{explicable, causal means} to bring about his purposes; in other words, there's no magic. See \hyperref[DC103-15]{DC 103:15--17}; 
					}
			to bring about his great and eternal purposes.
			And by very small means the Lord doth confound the wise
			and bringeth about the salvation of many souls.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:8} % *ALMA-37-8 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-8}

			And now it hath hitherto been wisdom in God
			that these things should be preserved.
			For behold, they have enlarged the memory of this people,
			yea, and convinced many of the error of their ways
			and brought them to the knowledge of their God,
			unto the salvation of their souls.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:9} % *ALMA-37-9 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-9}

			Yea, I say unto you:
			Were it not for these things that these records do contain, which are on these plates,
			Ammon and his brethren could not have convinced
			so many thousands of the Lamanites of the incorrect tradition of their fathers.
			Yea, these records and their words brought them unto repentance;
			that is, they brought them to the knowledge of the Lord their God
			and to rejoice in Jesus Christ their Redeemer.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:10} % *ALMA-37-10 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-10}

			And who knoweth but what they will be the means
			of bringing many thousands of them
			---yea, and also many thousands of our stiffnecked brethren, the Nephites,
			which are now hardening their hearts in sins and iniquities---
			to the knowledge of their Redeemer.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:11} % *ALMA-37-11 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-11}

			Now these mysteries are not yet fully made known unto me;
			therefore I shall forbear.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:12} % *ALMA-37-12 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-12}

			And it may suffice if I only say
			they are preserved for a wise purpose,
			which purpose is known unto God.
			For he doth counsel in wisdom over all his works,
			and his paths are straight,
			and his course is one eternal round.%
				\footnote{%
					% \label{}%
					For this phrase, see \hyperref[ROUND-PHRASES]{here}.
					}

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:13} % *ALMA-37-13 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-13}

			O remember, remember, my son Helaman,
			how strict is the commandments of God!
			And he saith:
			If ye will keep my commandments, ye shall prosper in the land.
			But if ye keep not his commandments, ye shall be cut off from his presence.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:14} % *ALMA-37-14 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-14}

			And now remember, my son,
			that God hath entrusted you with these things which are sacred,
			which he hath kept sacred,
			and also which he will keep and preserve for a wise purpose in him,
			that he may shew forth his power unto future generations.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:15} % *ALMA-37-15 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-15}

			And now behold, I tell you by the spirit of prophecy
			that if ye transgress the commandments of God,
			behold, these things which are sacred
			shall be taken away from you by the power of God;
			and ye shall be delivered up unto Satan,
			that he may sift you as chaff before the wind.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:16} % *ALMA-37-16 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-16}

			But if ye keep the commandments of God
			and do with these things which are sacred
			according to that which the Lord doth command you
			---for you must appeal unto the Lord
			for all things whatsoever ye must do with them---
			behold, no power of earth or hell can take them from you,
			for God is powerful to the fulfilling of all his words.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:17} % *ALMA-37-17 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-17}

			For he will fulfill all his promises which he shall make unto you,
			for he hath fulfilled his promise which he hath made unto our fathers.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:18} % *ALMA-37-18 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-18}

			For he promised unto them
			that he would preserve these things for a wise purpose in him,
			that he might shew forth his power unto future generations.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:19} % *ALMA-37-19 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-19}

			And now behold, one purpose hath he fulfilled,
			even to the restoration of many thousands of the Lamanites
			to the knowledge of the truth.
			And he hath shewn forth his power in them.
			And he will also still shew forth his power in them unto future generations.
			Therefore they shall be preserved.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:20} % *ALMA-37-20 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-20}

			Therefore I command you, my son Helaman,
			that ye be diligent in fulfilling all my words
			and that ye be diligent in keeping the commandments of God
			as they are written.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:21} % *ALMA-37-21 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-21}

			And now I will speak unto you concerning those twenty four plates,
			that ye keep them,
			that the mysteries and the works of darkness and their secret works
			---or the secret works of those people which have been destroyed---
			may be made manifest unto this people
			---yea, all their murders and robbings and their plunderings
			and all their wickedness and abominations may be made manifest unto this people---
			yea, and that ye preserve these directors.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:22} % *ALMA-37-22 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-22}

			For behold, the Lord saw that his people began to work in darkness
			---yea, work secret murders and abominations---
			therefore the Lord said
			if they did not repent,
			they should be destroyed from off the face of the earth.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:23} % *ALMA-37-23 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-23}

			And the Lord said:
			I will prepare unto my servant Gazelem
			a stone which shall shine forth in darkness unto light,
			that I may discover unto my people which serve me---
			that I may discover unto them the works of their brethren,
			yea, their secret works, their works of darkness,
			and their wickedness and abominations.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:24} % *ALMA-37-24 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-24}

			And now my son, these directors were prepared
			that the word of God might be fulfilled which he spake, saying:

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:25} % *ALMA-37-25 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-25}

			I will bring forth out of darkness unto light
			all their secret works and their abominations.
			And except they repent, I will destroy them from off the face of the earth.
			And I will bring to light all their secrets and abominations
			unto every nation which shall hereafter possess the land.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:26} % *ALMA-37-26 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-26}

			And now my son, we see that they did not repent;
			therefore they have been destroyed.
			And thus far the word of God hath been fulfilled;
			yea, their secret abominations have been brought out of darkness
			and made known unto us.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:27} % *ALMA-37-27 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-27}

			And now my son, I command you
			that ye retain all their oaths and their covenants
			and their agreements in their secret abominations;
			yea, and all their signs and their wonders ye shall retain from this people,
			that they know them not,
			lest peradventure they should fall into darkness also and be destroyed.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:28} % *ALMA-37-28 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-28}

			For behold, there is a curse upon all this land,
			that destruction shall come upon all those workers of darkness,
			according to the power of God,
			when they are fully ripe.
			Therefore I desire that this people might not be destroyed.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:29} % *ALMA-37-29 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-29}

			Therefore ye shall keep
			these secret plans of their oaths and their covenants
			from this people.
			And only their wickedness and their murders and their abominations
			shall ye make known unto them.
			And ye shall teach them to abhor such wickedness and abominations and murders.
			And ye shall also teach them that those people were destroyed
			on account of their wickedness and abominations and their murders.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:30} % *ALMA-37-30 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-30}

			For behold, they murdered all the prophets of the Lord
			which came among them to declare unto them concerning their iniquities.
			And the blood of those which they murdered did cry unto the Lord their God
			for vengeance upon those which were their murderers.
			And thus the judgments of God did come
			upon them workers of darkness and secret combinations.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:31} % *ALMA-37-31 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-31}

			Yea, and cursed be the land forever and ever
			unto those workers of darkness and secret combinations, even unto destruction,
			except they repent before they are fully ripe.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:32} % *ALMA-37-32 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-32}

			And now my son, remember the words which I have spoken unto you.
			Trust not those secret plans unto this people,
			but teach them an everlasting hatred against sin and iniquity.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:33} % *ALMA-37-33 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-33}

			Preach unto them repentance and faith on the Lord Jesus Christ.
			Teach them to humble themselves and to be meek and lowly in heart.
			Teach them to withstand every temptation of the devil,
			with their faith on the Lord Jesus Christ.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:34} % *ALMA-37-34 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-34}

			Teach them to never be weary of good works,
			but to be meek and lowly in heart,
			for such shall find rest to their souls.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:35} % *ALMA-37-35 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-35}

			O remember, my son, and learn wisdom in thy youth;
			yea, learn in thy youth to keep the commandments of God.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:36} % *ALMA-37-36 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-36}

			Yea, and cry unto God for all thy support.
			Yea, let all thy doings be unto the Lord.
			And whithersoever thou goest,
			let it be in the Lord.
			Yea, let all thy thoughts be directed unto the Lord.
			Yea, let the affections of thy heart be placed upon the Lord forever.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:37} % *ALMA-37-37 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-37}

			Counsel the Lord in all thy doings,
			and he will direct thee for good.
			Yea, when thou liest down at night,
			lie down unto the Lord,
			that he may watch over you in your sleep.
			And when thou risest in the morning,
			let thy heart be full of thanks unto God.
			And if ye always do these things,
			ye shall be lifted up at the last day.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:38} % *ALMA-37-38 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-38}

			And now my son, I have somewhat to say
			concerning the thing which our fathers call a ball or director
			---or our fathers called it Liahona,
			which is being interpreted a compass---
			and the Lord prepared it.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:39} % *ALMA-37-39 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-39}

			And behold, there cannot any man work
			after the manner of so curious a workmanship.
			And behold, it was prepared to shew unto our fathers
			the course which they should travel in the wilderness.

		% --
		\paragraph{Alma 37:40} % *ALMA-37-40
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-40}

			And it did work for them according to their faith in God.
			Therefore if they had faith to believe
			that God could cause that those spindles%
				\footnote{%
					\label{ALMA-37-40-NOTE-spindles}%
					See \hyperref[1NEPHI-16-10-NOTE-spindles]{this note}.
					}
			should point the way they should go,
			behold, it was done.
			Therefore they had this miracle
			---and also many other miracles---
			wrought by the power of God day by day.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:41} % *ALMA-37-41 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-41}

			Nevertheless, because those miracles were worked by small means
			---nevertheless it did shew unto them marvelous works---
			they were slothful and forgat to exercise their faith and diligence.
			And then those marvelous works ceased,
			and they did not progress in their journey.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:42} % *ALMA-37-42 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-42}

			Therefore they tarried in the wilderness,
			or did not travel a direct course,
			and were afflicted with hunger and thirst because of their transgression.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:43} % *ALMA-37-43 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-43}

			And now my son, I would that ye should understand
			that these things are not without a shadow.
			For as our fathers were slothful to give heed to this compass
			---now these things were temporal---
			they did not prosper;
			even so it is with things which are spiritual.

		% ---
		\paragraph{Alma 37:44} % *ALMA-37-44 
		% ---
			\label{ALMA-37-44}

			For behold, it is as easy to give heed to the word of Christ,
